### E01-01 · 十四平米出租屋卧室（深夜手机屏光） · 12.7 秒 · 4 个分镜

> 走位：苏晚居中偏画面右侧平躺，床尾在画面左侧；无第二人物入画；画面主体为苏晚与手机光。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 2.5 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 凌晨两点，苏晚侧躺在床上刷手机，眼皮越来越沉，头一点一点往下坠。 | 苏晚 |
| #2 | 2.50s | 2.5 | 特写 | Push In（推） | — | 她抬手去按灭屏幕，床底慢慢伸出一只苍白的手，轻轻抓住她的脚踝。 | 苏晚 |
| #3 | 5.00s | 3.4 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 苏晚：「……谁。」 苏晚整个人僵住，手机还亮着，屏幕的光打在天花板上。 | 苏晚 |
| #4 | 8.40s | 4.3 | 大特写 | Tilt Down（俯摇） | — | 她慢慢低下头，看见床沿外那只手，皮肤薄得透出青色。 苏晚：「床底下……有人。」 | 苏晚 |

```text

integrated_multimodal_description:
[Shot 1] <Picture 1>: in a static shot, A 26-year-old Chinese woman with dark circles and a weary face lies on her side in bed in a dim 14-square-meter rental bedroom, scrolling her phone; the phone screen lights up her face, and her head nods lower and lower in drowsiness.
[Shot 2] At 00:02.500, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: in a slow push in, a pale thin hand reaches out slowly from under the bed and gently grips her ankle.
[Shot 3] At 00:05.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: in a static shot, the woman freezes completely, eyes wide, the phone still glowing, light falling on the ceiling; she whispers <d>[Chinese]……谁。</d> in a voice barely audible (S1, a 26-year-old Chinese woman, hoarse and frightened).
[Shot 4] At 00:08.400, the camera cuts to <Picture 4>: with a slow tilt down, she slowly lowers her head to look at the pale thin hand at the edge of the bed, then says in a trembling voice <d>[Chinese]床底下……有人。</d> (S1).

overall_soundscape: Faint electrical hum of the phone, soft rustle of the blanket, a single slow drip of water in the dark, heavy nervous breathing.

non_diegetic_music: Low sustained drone with sparse piano notes, very quiet, building unease.
```
